# q-fin.CP [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Global Multi-Maturity SPX-VIX Calibration Beyond Markovian Stitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04087)
*Atithi Acharya,Yue Sun,Brandon Augustino,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Shree Hari Sureshbabu,Charlie Che*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于S&P 500 (SPX)和VIX微笑曲面联合校准的全局框架，覆盖多个期限，无需马尔可夫拼接的条件独立性限制。核心贡献包括：证明了局部与全局可行性等价（通过保块SPX马尔可夫化）；展示了拼接定律是全局可行路径定律的严格子集；提出增广Bregman镜像下降方案，在保持可观测报价矩拟合的同时控制鞅和离散残差，数值实验表明该方法在不可行仿射系统中将边际分布约束约束得更紧（约25倍），并在真实SPX/VIX曲面上展示了良好的校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多期限SPX-VIX微笑联合校准方法通常依赖马尔可夫拼接技术，这引入了条件独立性限制，无法处理依赖早期历史信息的多周期路径定律。作者希望建立一个更一般的全局框架，能够准确刻画可实现的联合定律空间，并开发高效的数值算法来逼近这一空间。

Method: 1) 理论部分：建立块结构框架，证明局部与全局可行性等价——每个全局可行定律都有一个保块的SPX马尔可夫化，保持每月(S_i,V_i,S_{i+1})定律不变；但拼接定律是全局可行路径定律的严格子集，因为马尔可夫化丢弃了超出当前SPX水平的早期历史依赖。2) 信息理论：在标准马尔可夫参照下，相对熵选择拼接的最小信息补全。3) 数值方法：提出增广Bregman镜像下降方案，用于有限离散化问题，在拟合报价矩的同时控制鞅和离散残差。

Result: 在受控的不可行仿射系统中，该方法通过暴露条件行的不一致性，将规定的边际分布约束保持得比循环行投影紧约25倍。精确有限状态示例验证了块保留性质，并展示了马尔可夫化后实质性的跨期价格变化。在平滑SPX和VIX曲面上，最差拟合微笑误差在整个扫描范围内保持在0.70波动率点以下，同时批量条件诊断指标大幅改善。

Conclusion: 作者建立了SPX-VIX多期限联合校准的更一般理论框架，揭示了马尔可夫拼接方法在路径定律空间上的局限性（即跨期依赖信息无法仅通过相邻微笑识别），并提供了理论可行的数值算法。实际含义是：如果定价多周期衍生品，仅匹配月度微笑是不够的，需要额外的跨期信息、适当的目标函数或历史依赖先验。

Abstract: We develop a global framework for joint S&P 500 (SPX)-VIX smile calibration across multiple maturities without the conditional-independence restriction induced by Markovian stitching. Exact local and global feasibility are equivalent: every globally feasible law has a block-preserving SPX-Markovization that leaves each monthly $(S_i,V_i,S_{i+1})$ law unchanged. Nevertheless, stitched laws can form a strict subset of globally feasible path laws because Markovization discards dependence on earlier history beyond the current SPX level. Adjacent smiles therefore cannot identify this dependence, and laws with identical monthly calibrations can price multi-period claims differently. Under the standard Markov reference, relative entropy selects the stitched minimum-information completion; non-Markov dependence requires cross-period information, an appropriate objective, or a history-dependent prior. For finite discretizations, we introduce an augmented-Bregman mirror-descent scheme. It preserves the fit to observable quote moments while controlling martingale and dispersion residuals. In a controlled infeasible affine system, this split keeps prescribed marginals about $25$ times tighter than cyclic row projection by exposing the discrepancy in the conditional rows. An exact finite-state example verifies block preservation and exhibits material cross-period price changes after Markovization. On smoothed SPX and VIX surfaces, numerical calculations illustrate a finite-budget penalty path: the worst fitted-smile error remains below $0.70$ volatility points across the reported sweep while the bulk conditional diagnostics improve substantially.

</details>

### [175] [Mean-field equilibrium of heterogeneous agents under market impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03115)
*Joseph Leclère,Mathieu Rosenbaum*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个线性均场模型，研究市场参与者在不同预测视界下基于共同信息做决策，其集体头寸通过市场冲击影响价格，最终价格收敛于鞅分量，可预测信号与市场冲击效应相互抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市场参与者虽然共享信息集，但预测视界异质，而市场冲击取决于总头寸而非交易者身份，这形成价格反馈回路。现有模型未充分刻画这种异质视界下集体决策对价格的均场反馈机制。

Method: 构建线性均场模型：价格分解为鞅分量、Volterra过程表示的共同可预测信号、以及总头寸产生的市场冲击三部分。在Gaussian-Volterra框架下，通过线性不动点方程刻画均衡的总头寸，建立存在唯一性条件，并研究完全考虑市场冲击的极限情形以及分数型信号与Gamma分布视界的局部Hölder正则性。

Result: 在均衡处识别出一个平衡条件，可消除共同信号对价格的直接传导。在适当缩放的均衡族极限中，可预测信号与市场冲击贡献相互抵消，观测价格收敛于其鞅分量。对分数型信号与Gamma视界分布，给出了局部Hölder界，并识别了使价格局部正则性与布朗运动相容的视界分布。

Conclusion: 异质视界下集体头寸的市场冲击效应可以在均衡中抵消可预测信号的直接传导，使价格渐近成为鞅；视界分布的选择决定观测价格能否呈现与布朗运动兼容的局部正则性。

Abstract: Although market participants generally have access to a common information set, they make decisions based on forecasts formed over heterogeneous horizons. Because market impact depends on aggregate positions rather than trader identities, these decisions feed back into prices through their collective effect. We introduce a linear mean-field model of this interaction. The observed price is decomposed into a martingale component, a common predictable signal represented by a Volterra process, and the market impact generated by aggregate positions. Agents take positions according to conditional forecasts of future signal increments and a fraction of anticipated aggregate impact over their respective horizons. Within a Gaussian-Volterra framework, we characterize equilibrium through a linear fixed-point equation for aggregate positions and establish existence and uniqueness under explicit conditions. At equilibrium, we identify a balance condition that cancels the direct transmission of the common signal to the observed price. We then study the limit in which agents fully account for market impact. Along a suitably scaled family of equilibria satisfying explicit conditions, the contributions of the predictable signal and the market impact cancel in the limit, and the observed price converges to its martingale component. For fractional-type signals and Gamma-distributed horizons, we further derive local Hölder bounds and identify the horizon distributions for which the observed price has local regularity compatible with that of Brownian motion.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Hosting Capacity Assessment of Data Centers with Voltage Ride-Through Capability in Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03030)
*Pengyu Ren,Wei Sun,Fei Teng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种电压穿越(VRT)感知的数据中心与电网协同规划框架，通过耦合输电网故障仿真与数据中心内部穿越模型，揭示了VRT能力可成为数据中心并网的约束性条件，仅靠稳态规划会高估可承载容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心作为集中的电力电子负荷，在电压扰动期间突然断开或转移至备用电源会移除大量需求，可能引发系统级稳定性问题。其并网可行性不仅取决于稳态热极限和电压极限，还取决于内部电力变换系统能否在维持IT服务的同时限制用户引发的负荷削减。

Method: 构建VRT感知的数据中心与电网协同规划框架：1) 基于Python的动态仿真生成IEEE 118节点系统在选定网络故障下的并网点(POI)电压轨迹；2) 所得电压波形驱动内部模型，该模型包含IT与冷却负荷动态、直流母线、UPS响应及变换器视在功率限制；3) 评估不同规划方案下的承载容量。

Result: IEEE 118节点算例表明：1) 内部VRT能力可成为具有约束性的并网约束，仅靠稳态规划会高估数据中心可行容量；2) 增加UPS变换器余量可逐步恢复承载容量；3) 在所研究的降阶响应模型下，构网(grid-forming)模式比限流构网(grid-following)模式提供更大的穿越裕度；4) VRT约束会实质性改变数据中心总承载容量及其在各候选并网母线上的空间分配。

Conclusion: 电压穿越约束在数据中心并网规划中不可忽略，需将内部穿越能力纳入协同规划；UPS变换器余量设计及构网型控制模式可有效提升穿越裕度和承载容量。

Abstract: Large data centers are emerging as concentrated, power-electronic grid loads whose abrupt disconnection or transfer to on-site backup supply during voltage disturbances can remove large demand from the power system, and may create a system-level stability problem. Their interconnection feasibility therefore depends not only on steady-state thermal and voltage limits, but also on whether internal power-conditioning systems can maintain IT service while limiting customer-initiated load reduction. This paper presents a voltage ride-through (VRT)-aware data center and grid co-planning framework that couples transmission-level fault simulation with an internal data center ride-through model. Python-based dynamic simulations generate point-of-interconnection (POI) voltage trajectories under selected network faults, and the resulting waveforms drive an internal model incorporating IT and cooling-load dynamics, DC-link, Uninterruptible Power Supply (UPS) response, and converter apparent power limits. The IEEE 118-bus case study shows that internal VRT capability can become a binding interconnection constraint: steady-state planning alone can overestimate feasible data center capacity, whereas increased UPS converter headroom progressively restores hosting capacity. Under the reduced-order response models studied, the grid-forming mode provides greater ride-through margin than the current-limited grid-following mode under the same network fault conditions. The results further show that VRT constraints can materially change both the total hosting capacity of data centers and its spatial allocation across candidate interconnection buses.

</details>


### [177] [Dynamic Operational Reserve Margin Assessment from Risk-Constrained Unit Commitment States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03066)
*Pratishtha Shukla,Shaked Regev,Evan J. R. Brody,Charles Foltz,Teja Kuruganti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出动态储备裕度（DRM）作为一种时变运行充裕度指标，源自风险约束机组组合（RCUC）状态，用于量化5分钟响应窗口内可用发电容量相对不确定性和事故储备需求的充裕程度。同时提出储备风险包络（RRE）指标以MW单位量化运行储备余量，并开发低裕度持续时间指标衡量储备压力的持续性。通过IEEE 14节点算例和大规模RCUC案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储备裕度指标为静态或平均化指标，无法捕捉运行条件变化下储备可达性（ramp-accessible capability）的时变特征以及储备压力的持续程度，难以真实反映系统在面临不确定性和事故时的储备充裕性。

Method: 基于风险约束机组组合（RCUC）状态推导时变DRM指标；提出RRE指标以可解释的MW术语量化运行储备裕量；开发低裕度持续时间指标衡量储备应力在运行时域上的持续性；在IEEE 14节点系统及大规模算例中进行多运行场景验证。

Result: 结果表明储备需求和爬坡可达储备能力在不同运行条件下显著变化；机组组合决策能够自适应调整以维持储备充裕性；DRM和RRE指标能揭示传统储备裕度指标无法捕捉的储备可达性和压力持续性问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的DRM和RRE指标提供了可解释的运行储备充裕度表征，弥补了传统储备裕度指标在储备可达性和压力持续性刻画上的不足，为电力系统运行储备评估提供了更精细的时变量化工具。

Abstract: We propose Dynamic Reserve Margin (DRM) as a time-varying operational adequacy metric derived from risk-constrained unit commitment (RCUC) states. DRM quantifies reserve adequacy using the additional generation capacity that committed generators can provide within a 5-minute response window relative to uncertainty and contingency reserve requirements. We introduce a complementary Reserve Risk Envelope (RRE) metric to quantify operational reserve headroom in directly interpretable MW terms. A low-margin duration metric is further developed to quantify the persistence of reserve stress over an operating horizon. We use an IEEE 14-bus example to illustrate these concepts, followed by large-scale RCUC case studies under multiple operating scenarios. Results demonstrate that reserve requirements and ramp-accessible reserve capability can vary substantially across operating conditions, and that commitment decisions adapt to maintain reserve adequacy under changing system conditions. The proposed DRM and RRE metrics provide an interpretable operational characterization of reserve adequacy, reveal reserve accessibility and stress persistence that are not captured by conventional reserve margin metrics.

</details>


### [178] [Finite-Sample Limits of Entropy-Based Structure Identification in Discretized Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03074)
*Pratishtha Shukla,James Nutaro*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了模糊归纳推理（FIR）框架中，离散化如何从根本上限制随机系统的结构识别能力。作者引入"分辨率-随机性比率"来刻画熵基输入选择何时可靠，并给出三个理论结果：熵基选择在阈值以下一致但在阈值以上失效（与样本量无关）；用熵基变量预测相比MSE基变量有闭式超额风险；识别因果相关输入所需数据随输入组合数增长并随熵信号强度反比衰减。理论在双状态马尔可夫模型和配电网可靠性数据集上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机系统中，当随机性超过离散化分辨率时，基于熵的方法无法区分哪个输入真正驱动输出。这严重制约了因果结构识别，而现有工作对此基础性限制缺乏系统理论分析。论文旨在揭示这一根本瓶颈，并量化熵基选择与MSE基选择在解释性和预测性之间的权衡。

Method: 论文在FIR（模糊归纳推理）这一非参数框架下建立理论分析。引入"分辨率-随机性比率"作为关键量，刻画熵基输入选择的可靠区间；通过对比熵基（针对可解释性）与MSE基（针对预测）两种变量选择准则，推导熵基选择的一致性和超额预测风险的闭式表达式，并给出因果输入识别所需样本量的标度律。理论在双状态马尔可夫模型上验证，并在配电网可靠性数据集上做实例应用。

Result: 三个主要结果：(1) 熵基选择在分辨率-随机性阈值以下保持一致，但超过该阈值后无论样本量多大都丧失判别力；(2) 用熵选变量预测相比MSE选变量，存在闭式超额预测风险，该风险随输入复杂度增大、随样本量减小；(3) 可靠识别因果相关输入所需数据量随输入组合数呈线性增长，并与熵信号强度成反比。理论在仿真和配电网实例（评估基础设施投资对可靠性的影响）中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了一个本质性限制：在随机性超过离散化分辨率时，因果可解释性（熵基）与预测精度（MSE基）存在不可调和的权衡，且熵基因果选择存在样本无关的失效阈值。这意味着在强随机系统中，增加采样数据无法弥补离散化分辨率不足带来的结构识别困难，为熵基方法在结构识别中的应用划定了明确的适用边界，并给出了实际中评估其可靠性的指导准则。

Abstract: Discretization fundamentally limits structure identification in stochastic systems. When system stochasticity exceeds the discretization resolution, entropy-based methods lose their ability to distinguish which input drives the output. We study this in Fuzzy Inductive Reasoning (FIR), a nonparametric framework for learning dynamical systems from discretized measurements, where the choice of input variables determines both predictive accuracy and the interpretability of the learned input--output relationships. Entropy-based selection targets explainability, i.e., identifying which variables causally drive the output, while mean-squared-error-based selection targets prediction. We introduce a resolution-stochasticity ratio that governs when entropy-based selection is reliable. Three results follow. First, entropy-based selection is consistent below this threshold but loses discriminative power above it, regardless of sample size. Second, using the entropy-selected variables for prediction instead of the MSE-selected ones incurs a closed-form excess prediction risk that grows with input complexity and shrinks with sample size. Third, reliable identification of the causally relevant inputs requires data that scales with the number of input combinations and inversely with the strength of the entropy signal. The theory is validated on a two-state Markov model and demonstrated on a distribution grid reliability dataset analyzing the impact of infrastructure investment, where the goal is to explain which investments drive reliability improvements rather than merely predict outcomes.

</details>


### [179] [On a Gradation for Asymptotic Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03120)
*Yiğit Narter,Inigo Incer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出渐近稳定性的"度"（degree）概念，用于量化收敛速率。度为0对应指数稳定，度m>0对应代数衰减阶t^{-1/m}。提供了直接与逆Lyapunov测试、精确稳定度判定条件，并通过Hopf、Bautin、分数度及时变例子验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的渐近稳定性只保证收敛，但无法量化收敛速率。作者希望引入一种分级体系，在指数稳定与任意慢收敛之间提供精细的速率区分，从而更精确地刻画系统的稳定性质量。

Method: 引入渐近稳定性的"度"（degree）作为分级指标：度0对应指数稳定，度m>0对应t^{-1/m}阶代数衰减。提出直接和逆Lyapunov测试来判定可容许的度，并给出精确判定稳定度所需的条件。

Result: 通过Hopf、Bautin、分数度系统及时变系统等实例，展示了所提出的度如何识别系统的主导稳定机制，验证了该框架的有效性和可区分性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种比经典渐近稳定性更精细的收敛速率量化框架，能够区分不同衰减阶数并识别主导稳定机制，丰富了Lyapunov稳定性理论。

Abstract: Classical asymptotic stability guarantees convergence but does not quantify the rate at which convergence occurs. This paper introduces a gradation of asymptotic stability where degree zero corresponds to exponential stability and degree $m>0$ corresponds to algebraic decay of order $t^{-1/m}$. We provide direct and converse Lyapunov tests for admissible degrees and conditions for certifying the exact stability degree. Hopf, Bautin, fractional-degree, and time-varying examples demonstrate how the degree identifies the leading stabilizing mechanism.

</details>


### [180] [A Reconfigurable Hybrid Convolutional-Fully Connected Neuromorphic Core for Biomedical Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03174)
*Sarah Johari,Suman Kumar,Abhishek Mishra,Anush Lingamoorthy,Nagarajan Kandasamy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于FPGA的可编程脉冲卷积神经网络(SCNN)推理架构，面向实时缺氧分类这一生物医学边缘应用。该架构在可编程、量化、基于层的神经形态硬件核心上实现了混合脉冲卷积-全连接(CNN-FC)拓扑，并在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和基于PPG信号的缺氧分类任务上验证了其性能，缺氧分类达到88.26%的准确率，动态功耗仅1.455W。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学边缘计算需要低功耗、实时响应的推理系统，传统CNN在边缘设备上计算开销大、功耗高。神经形态计算和脉冲神经网络(SNN)提供了一种能效更高的替代方案，但需要硬件架构支持其部署。

Method: 采用FPGA上的可编程量化硬件核心，实现混合拓扑：早期层通过感受野连接进行脉冲卷积（支持多通道卷积核和步长），深层采用全连接脉冲层进行分类。开发了基于PyTorch的软硬件协同设计流程，支持训练参数部署、量化和可配置性。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上分别达到98%和86%的硬件精度（16位量化）。缺氧分类任务中使用肩戴式传感器的红/红外PPG信号（肤色作为额外输入通道），五折交叉验证平均硬件准确率88.26%，动态功耗1.455W。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了低功耗神经形态生物医学边缘分类的可行性，展示了FPGA可编程SCNN架构在真实医疗场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: This work presents a programmable FPGA-based architecture for spiking convolutional neural network (SCNN) inference, with real-time hypoxia classification serving as a biomedical edge application. The architecture implements a hybrid spiking convolutional-fully connected (CNN-FC) topology on a programmable, quantized, layer-based neuromorphic hardware core. Early layers perform spiking convolution using receptive-field connectivity with support for multi-channel kernels and stride, while deeper layers use fully connected spiking layers for classification. A PyTorch-based hardware-software co-design flow enables deployment of trained parameters with quantization and configurability support. The design is first validated on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST, achieving hardware accuracies of up to 98% and 86%, respectively, at 16-bit precision. It is then applied to hypoxia classification using red and infrared photoplethysmography (PPG) signals acquired from a shoulder-mounted sensor, with skin tone included as an additional input channel. The resulting classifier achieves an average hardware accuracy of 88.26% across five folds at 16-bit precision while consuming 1.455 W of dynamic power, demonstrating the feasibility of low-power neuromorphic biomedical classification at the edge.

</details>


### [181] [Locating Power System Oscillation Sources by Extracting Interharmonics from Synchrophasor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03228)
*Sophia Xu,Wilsun Xu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了能否直接从PMU同步相量数据中提取间谐波以用于振荡源定位。结果表明，对于某些振荡现象，相量数据可以支持间谐波提取和振荡源定位，并通过灵敏度研究确定了相关要求，开发了基于云的PMU源定位软件工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究证明振荡相量源于间谐波引起的拍频波，并据此提出了基于间谐波的振荡源定位方法，但该方法所需的波形数据不如PMU同步相量数据容易获取。本文旨在探究能否直接从相量数据中提取间谐波，以便利用更广泛可用的PMU数据进行类似的振荡源定位应用。

Method: 通过灵敏度研究分析从相量数据中提取间谐波的条件和要求，考察相量数据支撑间谐波提取和振荡源定位的可行性，并开发基于云计算的PMU源定位软件工具。

Result: 对于某些振荡现象，相量数据能够支持间谐波提取和振荡源定位；灵敏度研究识别出了具体的应用要求；成功开发了基于云的PMU源定位工具。

Conclusion: 相较于依赖波形数据的方法，直接基于PMU相量数据提取间谐波并进行振荡源定位是可行的（在特定条件下），扩展了间谐波方法在实际电力系统中的应用范围。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that oscillating phasors arise from beating waves caused by interharmonics. This finding has led to an interharmonic-based oscillation source-location method. But waveform data needed for the method are less available than PMU synchrophasor data. This paper investigates whether interharmonics can be extracted directly from phasor data for similar applications. The results show that, for certain oscillation phenomena, phasor data can support interharmonic extraction and oscillation source location. Sensitivity studies have identified the requirements, and a cloud-based software tool is developed for PMU-based source location.

</details>


### [182] [Trajectory-Based Co-Optimization of Arrival Scheduling and Descent Path Design in the Terminal Maneuvering Area](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03234)
*Yutian Pang,John-Paul Clarke*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 论文提出一个四维终端区进港调度器，将每条航班的横向路径延伸、下滑道截获距离和襟翼放出的触发速度集成在一个决策中联合优化，从而弥合传统进港调度与节能下降程序设计之间的鸿沟。通过离线六自由度前向仿真评估每个候选慢车下降方案，并在滚动时域调度器下结合尾流间隔与实测风场提交已验证的下降与延展方案。亚特兰大终端区两个案例研究表明，联合优化的连续下降在自由下降环境下可节省约15%机队燃油，延迟减速可节省约23%；在公布航路的8L跑道进港流中，航图高度限制使设计网格损失31%并将节省降至9–10%与20–21%。风对单机下降燃油影响达34–81%，但仅解释最多4%的机队燃油方差，因为个体风效应在场景中被平均抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进港调度与下降程序设计被两个独立文献分别研究：调度模型把飞机简化为航程时间并给出目标落地时刻，节油下降程序则逐机设计且以调度结果为给定前提。然而两者其实都在决定进港飞机在何处吸收延误，因此现有框架无法在"较慢但提前构型的下降"与"水平航迹英里"之间做权衡——时间高效的调度可能燃油昂贵，而自主或降员运行需要一个地面自动化与飞行管理系统都能接受的单一轨迹计划。

Method: 提出四维终端区进港调度器：在同一决策中选择每架飞机的横向路径延伸、下滑道截获距离及襟翼放出触发速度。离线阶段用风感知的反向规划和六自由度前向仿真评估每个候选慢车下降方案，返回下降时间、油耗、最小航迹长度与稳定进近可行性；滚动时域调度器在尾流间隔约束和观测到港风的条件下为每架飞机提交一个已验证的下降和一个延展方案。

Result: 亚特兰大两个终端区案例显示：自由下降环境下联合优化连续下降节省约15%机队燃油，延迟减速节省约23%；在6条已公布的Runway 8L进港流中，航图高度下限移除了31%的设计格点，使节省分别降至9–10%与20–21%。风可使单机下降燃油变化34–81%，但对机队燃油方差解释力至多4%，因个体风效应在场景间平均化。

Conclusion: 将航班级燃油尽可能纳入调度模型可带来可观机队级收益，但公布程序中的高度约束会显著压缩可行设计空间、削减部分收益；风对机队整体的影响因平均化而远小于对单机的影响，因此调度器应以场景平均风匹配为主而非逐机精确修正。

Abstract: Terminal arrival scheduling and descent procedure design are studied in two largely separate literatures. Scheduling models reduce each aircraft to a travel time and deliver target landing times, and fuel-efficient descent procedures are designed one aircraft at a time with the schedule taken as given, although both decide where an arriving aircraft absorbs delay before final approach. Existing formulations therefore cannot trade a slower, earlier-configuring descent against level track miles, a schedule that is efficient in time can be expensive in fuel, and autonomous or reduced-crew operations will need a single trajectory plan that ground automation and the flight management system both accept. To close this gap, we propose a four-dimensional terminal arrival scheduler that selects each aircraft's lateral path extension, glideslope-capture distance, and flap-deployment trigger speeds in one decision. We evaluate every candidate idle-thrust descent offline with a wind-aware backward plan and a six-degree-of-freedom forward simulation that returns descent time, fuel burn, minimum track length, and stabilized-approach feasibility, and a rolling-horizon scheduler commits one verified descent and one extension per aircraft under wake-separation constraints and observed entry winds. Two Atlanta terminal airspace case studies quantify the benefit. We show that co-optimized continuous descents save about 15\% of fleet fuel below saturation in a free-descent environment and that delayed deceleration saves about 23\%, while on the six published Runway 8L arrival flows the charted altitude floors remove 31\% of the design lattice and reduce the savings to 9--10\% and 20--21\%, respectively. We also find that wind moves single-aircraft descent fuel by 34--81\% yet explains at most 4\% of fleet fuel variance, because aircraft-specific wind effects average out across a scenario.

</details>


### [183] [Preference-Oriented Aggregation of Heterogeneous Distributed Energy Resources for Reserve Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03252)
*Jingguan Liu,Xiaomeng Ai,Shichang Cui,Xizhen Xue,Shengshi Wang,Jiakun Fang,Wei Yao,Jinyu Wen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向备用调度的偏好导向型分布式能源资源(DERs)聚合方法，通过将Minkowski和重构为多胞体投影问题处理维度异构，并通过引入调度偏好的分布式聚合-调度协调框架处理类型异构，从而减少灵活性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DER聚合方法对异构DER过于保守，主要面临两大挑战：1) 维度异构性使不同时间尺度的灵活性难以组合；2) 类型异构性中多样且不规则的DER曲线导致近似不准确，造成显著的灵活性损失。

Method: 针对维度异构，利用矩阵变换技术将Minkowski和重构为多胞体投影问题，在高维空间统一DERs后再投影回聚合可行域；针对类型异构，开发分布式聚合-调度协调框架，将备用调度偏好纳入聚合过程，捕捉最优备用调度中关键的主动聚合灵活性。

Result: 数值测试验证了该方法在同时应对维度异构与类型异构方面的有效性，并展示了其在高备用需求电力系统中的良好应用潜力。

Conclusion: 偏好导向的聚合方法能够显著减少异构DER聚合时的灵活性损失，为高备用需求电力系统的分布式资源调度提供更高效聚合方案。

Abstract: Aggregating distributed energy resources (DERs) aims to encode their collective flexibility into a single set for efficient grid dispatch. However, existing aggregation methods are overly conservative for heterogeneous DERs due to two main challenges: 1) dimensional heterogeneity, which complicates the combination of flexibilities across different time dimensions, and 2) type heterogeneity, where diverse and irregular DER profiles hinder accurate approximations, resulting in significant flexibility loss. To resolve these challenges, this paper propose a novel preference-oriented aggregation method for reserve dispatch. For dimensional heterogeneity, we extend existing techniques by reformulating the Minkowski sum as a polytope projection problem using a matrix transformation technique. By unifying DERs in a higher-dimensional space and projecting them back into the aggregate feasible region, the proposed technique effectively aggregates dimensionally heterogeneous DERs. For type heterogeneity, we further develop a distributed aggregation-dispatch coordination framework that incorporates reserve dispatch preferences into aggregation. This framework effectively captures the critical, active aggregate flexibility prioritized in optimal reserve dispatch, thereby significantly reducing the flexibility loss when aggregating type-heterogeneous DERs. Numerical tests validate the effectiveness of our method in addressing both heterogeneities and highlight its promising potential for power systems with high reserve requirements.

</details>


### [184] [Coupling-Aware Aggregation of Multi-Zone HVAC Loads under Uncertainty: A Two-level Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03253)
*Jingguan Liu,Han Jiang,Xiaomeng Ai,Shengshi Wang,Xizhen Xue,Shichang Cui,Jinming Hou,Jiakun Fang,Jinyu Wen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种考虑耦合感知的两层聚合框架，用于量化多区域HVAC负荷的聚合灵活性，并通过高斯消元、坐标变换及矩阵变换技术将高维热动力学转化为低维解析表达，最终实现高效、准确的聚合灵活性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合建筑HVAC负荷可解锁电力系统需求侧灵活性，但多区域耦合带来的复杂相互依赖和不确定性传播使得聚合灵活性的量化变得困难，需要解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出耦合感知的两层聚合框架：建筑层采用定制高斯消元和坐标变换技术，将高维热动力学转化为等效低维解析表达式；聚合层开发新的矩阵变换技术，在不同维度的多面体之间构建内逼近，生成高维多区域HVAC灵活性在功率子空间中的闭式图像，并将其重构为可分离的线性规划以高效确定最优聚合参数。

Result: 案例研究验证了所提出框架的有效性，展示了其准确性、可靠性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过建筑层降维和聚合层内逼近技术，成功实现了多区域HVAC聚合灵活性的高效、精确量化，兼顾了不确定性的传播分析与计算可处理性。

Abstract: Aggregating building heating, ventilation, and air-conditioning (HVAC) loads unlocks substantial demand-side flexibility for power systems. Yet multi-zone coupling creates intricate interdependencies and uncertainty propagation, complicating the quantification of aggregate flexibility. To address this issue, this paper proposes a coupling-aware two-level aggregation framework. At the building level, tailored Gaussian elimination and coordinate transformation techniques are employed to recast the high-dimensional thermal dynamics as an equivalent lower-dimensional analytical expression. This expression streamlines the subsequent aggregator-level stage by (i) clarifying the propagation of zone-level uncertainties to the building-level interface, (ii) decoupling intra-building multi-zone coupling from inter-building aggregation, and (iii) providing full-dimensional building-level flexibility sets that enable tractable reformulation. At the aggregator level, existing geometric aggregation approaches are generalized by a newly developed matrix-transformation technique. This technique effectively constructs inner approximations between polytopes of different dimensions, producing closed-form images of high-dimensional multi-zone HVAC flexibility in power subspace. The resulting inner approximation is then recast as a customized separatable linear program that efficiently determines the optimal aggregate parameters. Case studies validate the effectiveness of our framework, highlighting its accuracy, reliability, and scalability.

</details>


### [185] [On the Degree of Safety: Beyond Safe or Unsafe with Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03319)
*Ruoyu Lin,Fabio Pasqualetti,Magnus Egerstedt*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文指出有效控制障碍函数（CBF）的内部值与梯度可任意改变，不能独立于集合表示量化安全程度；可行域不可行性也分为本质不可行与表示不可行。为此提出不变性权威需求（IAD），一种与表示无关的安全程度度量，用于指导集合或执行器修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBF仅提供二元的"安全/不安全"指示，不能量化安全程度；且现有的候选度量（如内部值、梯度、约束可行性）均依赖于安全集的表示方式，无法提供与表示无关的、有意义的安全程度度量。

Method: 论文通过理论分析证明有效CBF的内部值和梯度可以任意改变；区分了本质不可行（安全集非受控不变）与表示不可行（CBF表示失败）；提出IAD（不变性权威需求）作为与表示无关的安全程度量化指标。

Result: 证明CBF内部值与梯度不构成表示无关的安全程度度量；可行性约束本身也不能独立量化安全程度；引入的IAD能够量化受控不变性所需的控制权威，并指导集合或执行器修复。

Conclusion: 安全程度的量化需要与安全集表示方式无关的度量，IAD为此提供了可行方案，弥补了传统CBF二元指示的不足。

Abstract: A valid control barrier function (CBF) certifies if its represented safe set can be rendered forward invariant, and the sign of its value indicates whether a state is safe or not, but it does not quantify a degree of safety beyond the binary indication. In this paper, we show that among valid CBFs representing the same safe set, interior values and gradients can be changed arbitrarily, so neither quantity determines a degree of safety that is independent of how the set is represented. We also show that whether a candidate CBF-based inequality constraint is feasible does not by itself quantify a degree of safety. In particular, infeasibility can occur either because the safe set is not controlled invariant or because the candidate CBF representation fails. This motivates our distinction between intrinsic and representational infeasibility. Finally, we introduce the invariance authority demand (IAD), a representation-independent degree of safety that quantifies the control authority required for controlled invariance and can be used to guide set or actuator repair.

</details>


### [186] [Traffic Congestion Control for ARZ Model with an Arbitrarily Large Input Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03345)
*Yidan Cao,Xiang Xu,Lu Liu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了Aw-Rascle-Zhang (ARZ) 交通模型在存在任意大输入时延时的镇定问题。将输入时延建模为传输PDE，把线性化ARZ模型（一个带近端反射的2×2双曲PDE系统）重构为3×3双曲PDE系统，并设计基于反步法的边界控制器实现时延补偿镇定。关键技术贡献包括：针对带时延的双曲PDE提出特征区域分块的核函数构造方法（满足两个边界约束并通过逐次逼近证明），以及利用小增益定理分析双曲PDE的输入状态稳定性（ISS）。两个仿真验证了所提控制器的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARZ交通流模型在现实中常面临控制输入时延问题，而该模型线性化后是一个带近端反射的2×2双曲PDE系统，与输入时延结合时会带来显著的分析困难。已有方法难以处理任意大的输入时延，因此需要新的镇定策略。

Method: 1) 将输入时延建模为传输PDE，将原系统重构为3×3双曲PDE系统；2) 设计反步变换将原系统映射到稳定的目标系统，从而构造时延补偿控制器；3) 对于核函数，提出特征区域分块（characteristic-region-wise）的构造方法，满足两个边界约束并通过逐次逼近完成证明；4) 利用小增益定理分析闭环系统的输入状态稳定性（ISS）。

Result: 通过两个仿真验证了所提时延补偿控制器的有效性：一个与无补偿的控制器进行对比，另一个使用真实交通车辆数据进行验证，均表明所提出的控制器能够有效镇定系统。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了ARZ交通模型在任意大输入时延下的镇定问题，所提出的基于反步法的时延补偿边界控制器在理论上通过ISS小增益定理保证了稳定性，并通过仿真验证了其在实际应用场景中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the stabilization problem for Aw-Rascle-Zhang (ARZ) traffic model in the presence of an arbitrarily large input delay. The linearized ARZ model is a $2 \times 2$ hyperbolic partial differential equation (PDE) system with proximal reflection, which introduces significant analytical challenges when combined with input delays. To tackle this problem, we propose a backstepping-based boundary controller capable of stabilizing the linearized ARZ model under these conditions. The input delay is modeled as a transport PDE, which reformulates the entire system into a $3 \times 3$ hyperbolic PDE system. A backstepping transformation is designed to map the original system into a stable target system, enabling the design of a delay-compensated controller. A key technical contribution of this work is that for hyperbolic PDEs with delays, we develop a characteristic-region-wise construction for kernel functions subject to two boundary constraints and close the proof via successive approximation. Another contribution is that we utilize the small-gain theorem for input-to-state stability (ISS) of hyperbolic PDEs. Two simulations are provided to illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed delay-compensated controller: one compares it with a controller without compensation, and the other employs real traffic vehicle data to validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [187] [Taylor-Informed Indirect Adaptive Predictive Control Using Jacobian-Frozen Affine Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03348)
*Tam W. Nguyen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于泰勒展开信息的新型间接自适应预测控制框架，针对非线性采样数据系统，利用Jacobian冻结的仿射预测器进行模型预测控制。通过有限泰勒展开近似非线性动态，结合递推最小二乘在线辨识多项式系数，并在每个采样时刻冻结Jacobian生成仿射预测模型。数值仿真表明高阶模型在远离展开点的工作点处能改善跟踪精度，且控制代价相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性采样数据系统的模型预测控制面临模型辨识复杂、计算负担高以及通用特征字典低效等挑战。现有方法往往忽略系统的物理结构信息，导致词典冗余、辨识效率低下。本文旨在利用泰勒展开的结构知识指导多项式字典的构建，从而在保证建模精度的同时降低辨识复杂度。

Method: 1. 利用有限泰勒展开近似采样非线性动态；2. 采用递推最小二乘在线辨识多项式系数；3. 每个采样时刻对辨识映射的Jacobian在当前工作点求值并在预测区间内冻结，构造仿射预测器用于MPC；4. 多项式字典设计：利用精确联合奇对称性消除偶次总阶单项式，进一步采用前向欧拉结构剪枝实现有意的模型降阶。

Result: 在不稳定非线性基准上的数值仿真比较了不同泰勒阶数的效果。结果表明，高阶模型在操作点远离展开点时能提升跟踪精度，同时保持相当的控制能量消耗。

Conclusion: 泰勒结构感知的降阶多项式字典结合Jacobian冻结仿射预测，为非线性和采样数据系统提供了一种高效且可复现的间接自适应预测控制方案，具有较好的精度与复杂度之间的平衡。

Abstract: This paper develops a Taylor-informed indirect adaptive predictive control framework for nonlinear sampled-data systems using Jacobian-frozen affine predictors. A finite Taylor expansion approximates the sampled nonlinear dynamics, and recursive least squares (RLS) identifies its polynomial coefficients online. At each sampling instant, the Jacobian of the identified map is evaluated at the current operating point and frozen over the prediction horizon, yielding an affine predictor for model predictive control. In contrast to generic nonlinear feature dictionaries, the implemented polynomial dictionary is a forward-Euler/Taylor-structure-informed reduced dictionary. Exact joint-odd symmetry eliminates even-total-degree monomials, whereas additional forward-Euler-informed pruning constitutes a deliberate model reduction. Numerical simulations on an unstable nonlinear benchmark compare different Taylor degrees. The results show that higher-order models improve tracking accuracy as the operating point moves farther from the expansion point while maintaining comparable control effort. The complete MATLAB implementation is publicly available to facilitate reproducibility.

</details>


### [188] [Taylor-Informed Predictive Cost Adaptive Control for Quadrotors with Online Gravity-Trim Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03351)
*Tam W. Nguyen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于泰勒展开的预测代价自适应控制（PCAC）方法用于四旋翼飞行器，并引入在线重力微调自适应。通过在标称悬停点对非线性动力学进行一阶、二阶和三阶展开，构建稀疏采样数据字典，采用带变速率遗忘的逐行递归最小二乘辨识。每一步将辨识得到的预测器在当前状态线性化，并在预测时域内固定其雅可比矩阵。辨识的垂直动力学还可估计飞行器质量和重力微调输入，从而消除固定标称重力补偿。仿真实验（包括突发载荷变化和激进螺旋线跟踪）表明，高阶预测器在保持标准PCAC框架的同时提升了预测和跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼飞行器在面临突发载荷变化等干扰时，传统基于固定标称重力补偿的控制方法性能会退化。现有自适应控制方法缺乏对高阶非线性动力学的充分利用，且预测控制器的辨识与更新策略有限。因此需要一种能够在线自适应辨识动力学、同时估计重力微调并利用高阶展开提升预测精度的控制方案。

Method: 1) 在标称悬停点对非线性动力学进行1-3阶泰勒展开，构建稀疏采样数据字典；2) 采用逐行递归最小二乘（RLS）结合变速率遗忘因子进行在线辨识；3) 每步将辨识出的预测器在当前状态线性化，并在预测时域内固定雅可比矩阵；4) 通过辨识垂直动力学估计质量和重力微调输入，替代固定标称重力补偿；5) 保持标准PCAC优化框架不变。

Result: 仿真实验表明：在突发载荷变化条件下，高阶（2阶、3阶）泰勒展开预测器相比低阶预测器在预测精度和跟踪性能上均有显著提升；同时该方法在保持标准PCAC公式化框架的前提下实现了对质量与重力微调的有效在线估计。

Conclusion: 泰勒展开高阶预测与重力微调自适应结合，能够在动态环境（如载荷突变）下有效提升四旋翼预测控制的鲁棒性和跟踪性能，且该方案与现有PCAC框架兼容，无需根本性改变控制结构。

Abstract: This paper develops Taylor-informed predictive cost adaptive control (PCAC) for quadrotors with online gravity-trim adaptation. First-, second-, and third-order expansions of the nonlinear dynamics about nominal hover define sparse sampled-data dictionaries for row-wise recursive least-squares identification with variable-rate forgetting. At each step, the identified predictor is linearized at the current state, and its Jacobian is fixed over the prediction horizon. The identified vertical dynamics also estimate the vehicle mass and gravity-trim input, eliminating fixed nominal gravity compensation. Simulations with an abrupt payload change and aggressive helix tracking show that the higher-order predictors improve prediction and tracking while preserving the standard PCAC formulation.

</details>


### [189] [Synchronization of directed hypergraphs with heterogeneities via dynamic coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03698)
*Aiwin Thomas Vadakkan,Pietro De Lellis,Mario di Bernardo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究有向超图中非线性智能体在动态扩散耦合及参数失配条件下的完全同步问题。作者证明只有当参数失配在横向动力学中等效于常数扰动时才能实现完全同步，并在此条件下推导了同步流形的不变性与局部稳定性条件，建立了适用于有向超图和动态耦合的主稳定函数框架。数值模拟在Lorenz振子超图和钉扎控制舆论动力学中验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实网络中存在三个及以上智能体间的交互，无法简化为成对耦合，因此超图是自然的建模框架。此前比例-积分（PI）耦合方案已知可在异构线性网络中实现一致性，但在非线性系统中通常只能实现有界同步而无法保证完全同步。作者希望探索在何种条件下，动态扩散耦合能够在参数失配的非线性超图系统中实现完全同步，并建立相应的理论分析框架。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明参数失配在横向动力学中结构等价于常数扰动是实现完全同步的必要条件；2) 在此条件下推导同步流形的不变性和局部稳定性条件；3) 针对带动态耦合（比例与积分项）的有向超图，构建主稳定函数（MSF）框架，分别处理比例层和积分超图层，并提供预测同步区域的谱准则；4) 数值模拟：使用Lorenz振子有向超图验证理论，并应用于非线性舆论动力学的钉扎控制问题。

Result: 数值模拟验证了理论预测：积分作用能够补偿比例层引起的失稳效应。在有向Lorenz振子超图中，动态扩散耦合实现了完全同步；在非线性舆论动力学的钉扎控制问题中，动态耦合在智能体存在异质性时仍实现了完全的领导者-跟随者共识。

Conclusion: 动态扩散耦合在参数失配的非线性有向超图系统中实现完全同步是可能的，但前提是失配效应在横向上等价于常数扰动。所提出的MSF框架为分析和设计比例-积分动态耦合超图系统提供了有效的谱工具，并在多个应用场景中展示了其适用性和有效性。

Abstract: Many real-world networks involve interactions among three or more agents that cannot be reduced to pairwise coupling, making hypergraphs a natural modeling framework. In this work, we study complete synchronization in directed hypergraphs of nonlinear agents with parameter mismatches under dynamic diffusive coupling. Although proportional-integral coupling schemes are known to achieve consensus in heterogeneous linear networks, their ability to enforce complete synchronization in nonlinear systems is more limited, generally yielding only bounded synchronization. We show that complete synchronization can be attained only when the effect of parameter mismatches is structurally equivalent, in the transverse dynamics, to a constant disturbance. Under this condition, we derive invariance and local stability conditions for the synchronization manifold and develop a Master Stability Function framework for directed hypergraphs with dynamic coupling. The proposed approach accounts for distinct proportional and integral hypergraph layers and provides spectral criteria for predicting synchronization regions. Numerical simulations on directed hypergraphs of Lorenz oscillators validate the theoretical predictions and show how the integral action can compensate destabilizing effects induced by the proportional layer. We further demonstrate the applicability of the framework to a pinning-control problem in nonlinear opinion dynamics, where dynamic diffusive coupling achieves complete leader-follower consensus in the presence of heterogeneity.

</details>


### [190] [Can Julia land on the Moon? On the development of a GNC simulation framework for the Argonaut lunar lander](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03843)
*Francesco Capolupo,Frederik Markus*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了欧洲空间局（ESA）在Argonaut月球着陆器项目中开发的基于Julia编程语言的月球着陆仿真框架ATLAS（Argonaut Tools for Landing Analysis and Simulation），该框架覆盖着陆下降全过程，集成高保真动力学模型、传感器和执行器模型及GNC算法，并与MATLAB/Simulink生态进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲空间局需要在Argonaut月球着陆器项目中开发用于设计分析GNC算法的仿真工具，同时评估是否可以采用Julia替代传统MATLAB/Simulink开发实践，以满足早期原型开发和大规模蒙特卡洛分析的需求。

Method: 采用模块化架构，在Julia中构建ATLAS仿真套件，集成高保真平移和旋转动力学、可变质量特性、推进剂晃荡效应、详细的传感器和执行器模型，以及飞行代表性GNC算法，并在多速率仿真环境中运行。

Result: 结果表明Julia为机构驱动的研究、早期设计研究和计算密集型闭环仿真提供了强大、灵活且高性能的环境，支持大规模并行化仿真和快速设计迭代。

Conclusion: Julia能够搭建早期原型开发和大规模蒙特卡洛分析之间的桥梁，在单一环境中实现全流程仿真，为ESA的GNC开发提供了一个有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: No, the Julia programming language cannot land on the Moon - but it can play a crucial role in designing and analysing the Guidance, Navigation, and Control (GNC) algorithms required for doing so. This paper presents the development of a lunar landing simulation framework implemented in Julia at the European Space Agency (ESA), within the Argonaut lunar lander programme. ATLAS (Argonaut Tools for Landing Analysis and Simulation) is a modular suite of analysis and simulation tools that cover the complete descent and landing phase of Argonaut, integrating high fidelity translational and rotational dynamics, varying mass properties, propellant sloshing, detailed sensor and actuator models, and flight-representative GNC algorithms within a multi-rate simulation environment. The framework is intended to bridge early-phase prototyping and large-scale Monte Carlo analysis within a single environment. This work evaluates the advantages and limitations of adopting Julia compared to established GNC development practices based on the MATLAB/Simulink ecosystem. The results show that Julia provides a powerful, flexible, and high-performance environment for agency-driven research, early-phase design studies, and computationally intensive closed-loop simulations enabling large-scale, parallelizable simulations and rapid design iteration cycles.

</details>


### [191] [Data-Based Clustering and Control of Similar Biological Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03921)
*Peilin Zhang,Antonis Papachristodoulou,Idris Kempf*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于相似性的框架，通过数据驱动的方法对动态相似的生物系统进行聚类，并采用层级领导者-跟随者控制架构，大幅减少了需要求解的控制问题数量，实现了异构生物系统（如基因表达）的可扩展且可靠控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络遗传控制虽在合成生物学等领域有重要应用，但微流控平台并行控制大量细胞群体时，计算负担和生物异质性限制了传统控制策略的可扩展性。

Method: 利用现有的数据驱动方法从输入输出数据中量化控制相关的相似性，无需显式系统辨识即可对具有相似动态的系统进行聚类；基于聚类结果构建层级领导者-跟随者控制架构，为每个簇设计单一控制器并应用于簇内所有成员；分析簇内闭环行为，提出保证聚类闭环系统良态的数据驱动条件。

Result: 在基因表达动态的仿真中验证了该方法，表明基于相似性的分组能够实现异构生物系统的可扩展且可靠控制，显著减少了需要解决的控制问题数量。

Conclusion: 相似性分组的控制框架为解决大规模异构生物系统的并行控制提供了高效可行的方案，兼具可扩展性与可靠性。

Abstract: Cybergenetic control of gene expression enables applications in synthetic biology, drug development, and biomanufacturing. Microfluidic platforms allow the parallel control of large cell populations. However, the resulting computational burden and intrinsic biological heterogeneity limit the scalability of conventional control strategies. In this work, we propose a similarity-based framework to reduce the computational requirement of controlling large numbers of dynamical systems. Building on existing data-driven methods for quantifying control-relevant similarity from input-output data, we cluster systems with similar dynamics without requiring explicit system identification. Based on this grouping, we develop a hierarchical leader-follower control architecture, where a single controller is designed for each cluster and applied to all members. This significantly reduces the number of control problems that need to be solved. Furthermore, we analyse the closed-loop behaviour within clusters and develop data-driven conditions under which the clustered closed-loop systems remain well-posed. The proposed approach is demonstrated in simulations of gene expression dynamics, showing that similarity-based grouping enables scalable and reliable control of heterogeneous biological systems.

</details>


### [192] [Quantifying Power to Voltage and Frequency Dynamics for Oscillation Propagation Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03929)
*Onur Alican,Dionysios Moutevelis,Marc Cheah-Mañe,Oriol Gomis-Bellmunt,Eduardo Prieto-Araujo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于线性分析的频域框架，用于表征含逆变器资源(IBR)的电力系统中各母线电压和各发电机组频率对振荡的灵敏度。该框架推导出两个定量指标——频率灵敏度指数(FSI)和电压灵敏度指数(VSI)，源自EMT线性模型的传递函数，并在修改后的IEEE 68节点测试系统中通过时域仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IBR并网引入多时间尺度动力学和振荡，可能传播至远处区域并危及系统安全运行。在大规模互联系统中，振荡源、频率和传播路径往往难以识别，因此需要能够在大频率范围内表征系统对各位置电压和频率振荡灵敏度的分析工具。

Method: 提出基于线性EMT电力系统模型的频域分析框架，通过建立有功/无功注入与网络中电压/频率变量之间的传递函数，从解析上推导出两个定量指标——FSI和VSI，用于量化系统对不同类型和频率振荡的灵敏度。

Result: 该方法应用于部分和完全IBR渗透下的改进IEEE 68节点基准系统，并通过Matlab/Simulink环境中基于线性和非线性模型的EMT时域仿真验证了准确性。

Conclusion: 提出的FSI和VSI指标能为振荡检测和传播分析提供见解，有助于在含高比例IBR的大规模互联系统中识别振荡的灵敏度特征。

Abstract: The integration of Inverter-Based Resources (IBRs) into power systems introduces multi-timescale dynamics and oscillations which may propagate to distant areas and endanger the safe system operation. These oscillations pose a significant challenge as their source, frequency, and propagation pathways are often challenging to identify in large interconnected systems, comprising numerous synchronous machines and IBRs. For the above reasons, analytical tools that identify the sensitivity of the system to oscillations within a large frequency spectrum, affecting both voltage and frequency variables across various network locations, are of interest. This paper addresses this topic by introducing a frequency-domain framework based on linear analysis, which characterizes the sensitivity to oscillations of each network bus voltage and of each generation unit frequency. Within this framework, two quantitative indicators are proposed, namely the Frequency Sensitivity Index (FSI) and Voltage Sensitivity Index (VSI). These indexes are derived analytically from the transfer functions which relate the active and reactive power injections to each bus with the voltage and frequency variables across the network, derived from the linear Electromagnetic Transient (EMT) power system model. The proposed indices quantify the sensitivity of the system to oscillations of different type and frequency, providing insights for both oscillation detection and propagation analysis. The methodology is applied to a case study based on the modified IEEE 68-bus benchmark system under partial and full IBR penetration, while its accuracy is validated through EMT time-domain simulations using linear and nonlinear models developed in Matlab/Simulink environment.

</details>


### [193] [Formation Matrix and Energy-based Control of Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04158)
*Martín Crespo,Sergio Junco,Matías Nacusse*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量的控制器，用于多智能体机器人系统在平面上运动时实现并保持特定编队，同时避免智能体间碰撞。控制器模拟弹簧-阻尼器网络（以键合图建模），引入形成矩阵描述智能体间距离和相对速度，与IDA-PBC理论的control-by-interconnection方案对应，在端口哈密顿系统框架下求解编队控制问题，并给出了领导者跟随和基于位置的编队控制系统及稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统的编队控制是一个重要的研究课题，现有方法往往缺乏统一的能量框架来同时处理编队保持、碰撞避免和稳定性保证等问题。作者希望通过将编队控制问题纳入端口哈密顿系统框架，利用互联-阻尼分配无源控制理论的能量整形方法，提供一种系统化的控制设计途径。

Method: 1) 设计能量基控制器，模拟连接智能体对的弹簧-阻尼器模块网络；2) 使用键合图模型全面封装系统动力学；3) 通过图论方法引入形成矩阵，描述智能体间距离和相对速度；4) 将该矩阵与IDA-PBC理论的CbI（control-by-interconnection）方案对应，在端口哈密顿框架下求解编队控制；5) 提出基于CbI方案的领导者跟随和基于位置的编队控制，并进行闭环稳定性分析。

Result: 通过数值模拟在多种场景下验证了理论发现，表明所提出的控制方案能够有效实现编队形成与保持、避免碰撞，并且闭环系统具有稳定性保证。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将键合图建模、图论形成矩阵与端口哈密顿/IDA-PBC理论相融合，为多智能体编队控制提供了统一且物理意义清晰的能量方法框架，兼具编队保持、碰撞避免和稳定性的理论保证。

Abstract: This paper presents an energy-based controller for a multiagent robotic system designed to achieve and maintain a specific formation while moving on a plane and avoiding collisions between agents. The controller emulates a network of elementary spring-damper modules connecting pairs of agents. This network, with its de-energized states representing the desired formation, determines the system's dynamics, which is fully encapsulated by a bond graph model. The modeling is further enhanced through the introduction of a formation matrix, using a graph-theoretic approach, that describes both the distances and relative velocities among the agents of the arrangement. This matrix mathematically represents the interconnection and energy-exchange structure of the bond graph, allowing us to put it in correspondence with the control-by-interconnection CbI-scheme of the IDA-PBC theory, facilitating the solution of the formation control problem within the port-Hamiltonian system framework. Furthermore, the paper presents leader-following and position-based formation control systems based on the CbI scheme, including a stability analysis of the corresponding closed-loop systems. The theoretical findings are validated through numerical simulations across various scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Open Problems in AI Risk Modeling: Insights from a Workshop on the Technical Foundations of AI Risk Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03178)
*Krystal Jackson,Deepika Raman,Jakub Kryś,Sean P. Fillingham,Jack Kengott,Andrew J. Lohn,Nada Madkour,Henry Papadatos,James Sykes,Anna Katariina Wisakanto,Malcolm Murray*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了先进AI系统社会风险建模的设计问题，识别了当前阻碍风险建模应用的方法论与制度挑战，回顾了五大研究传统，比较了两种主流方案，并基于22位专家研讨会提出了一套开放问题议程与优先发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管越来越多监管提案要求进行系统性风险评估，但缺乏严格的定量方法，使得风险建模在实践中应呈现何种形态仍是未解问题。

Method: 回顾概率风险评估、灾难性AI风险分析、网络安全风险量化、贝叶斯因果推断和阈值治理五大研究传统；比较情景化风险估计与贝叶斯网络阈值设定两种方案；通过22位专家研讨会及后续分析提炼开放问题。

Result: 提出涵盖模型结构、范围、证据整合、验证与治理的结构化开放问题议程，并指出进展取决于定量建模与独立评估、透明分层披露及可持续维护风险模型的制度机构相结合。

Conclusion: 先进AI系统风险建模的实质性进展不能仅依赖定量方法本身，必须与独立评估机制、透明披露制度以及能够长期维护和更新模型的治理机构协同推进。

Abstract: We investigate the design of robust risk models to assess societal risks posed by advanced AI systems, an emerging area in AI governance. Many regulatory proposals increasingly require systemic risk assessment, but in the absence of rigorous quantitative methods, the question remains what state of the art risk modeling should look like in practice. We identify the key methodological and institutional challenges that currently limit the adoption of risk modeling. We review five research traditions that inform this problem: probabilistic risk assessment, catastrophic AI risk analysis, cybersecurity risk quantification, Bayesian causal inference, and threshold-based governance. We compare two leading proposals, scenario-based risk estimation and Bayesian network-based threshold setting. Drawing on a workshop with 22 experts and subsequent analysis, we identify a structured agenda of open questions concerning model structure, scope, evidence integration, validation, and governance. We close by outlining priorities for progress, arguing that it will depend on integrating quantitative modeling with independent evaluation, transparent and tiered disclosure, and institutions capable of maintaining and updating risk models over time.

</details>


### [195] [Reducing Catastrophic Risk from AI with Systematic Monitoring and Evaluation of Rogue AI Progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03189)
*T. Bauer,W. P. Kegelmeyer,E. Begoli,A. Sadovnik,T. Emerson,C. Corley,N. Generous,J. Moore,B. Bartoldson,R. Goldhan,M. Goldman,M. Greaves,M. J. D. Vermeer,B. MacLennan,D. Schulker,N. VanHoudnos,J. Bansemer,Y. Bengio*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套结构化的行为指标框架，用于预警人工智能系统可能向灾难性威胁方向发展的信号。该框架借鉴网络安全和国家安全的成熟方法论，通过跨维度设定明确的指标与阈值，支持研究者和政策制定者实施基于证据的监控协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统能力不断增强，其可能引发的灾难性威胁缺乏系统性的早期预警机制。现有的安全框架往往流于定性讨论或事后应对，缺少可操作性、可量化且基于证据的监控标准，因此需要借鉴网络安全和国家安全领域的实践来建立前瞻性的风险指标体系。

Method: 采用务实的分析方法，借鉴网络安全与国家安全领域已经验证的方法论，在AI能力与行为的多维度上建立清晰的度量标准（metrics）、指标（indicators）和阈值（thresholds），从而形成一套结构化的行为指标框架。

Result: 该框架为研究者和政策制定者提供了可实施的、基于证据的监控协议，使得AI的灾难性风险进展可以被系统性地识别和追踪。

Conclusion: 通过建立清晰的多维度指标和阈值体系，该框架填补了AI灾难性威胁早期预警领域的空白，为前瞻性风险治理提供了实用工具，但具体指标和阈值的有效性仍需实践检验。

Abstract: This article presents a structured framework of behavioral indicators that may signal progression toward potentially catastrophic threats from artificial intelligence systems. We adopt a pragmatic approach, inspired by established methodologies in cybersecurity and national security. By establishing clear metrics, indicators, and thresholds across multiple dimensions of AI capability and behavior, this framework enables researchers and policymakers to implement evidence-based monitoring protocols.

</details>


### [196] [Affective publics in Arabic YouTube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03269)
*Lynnette Hui Xian Ng,Craig Douglas Albert,Abdullah Melhem,Ahmed Aleroud,Lance Y. Hunter*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文分析了来自也门、沙特、伊拉克、约旦和叙利亚等五个阿拉伯国家的67,725条YouTube评论，发现阿拉伯YouTube的情感公众是一个共享的区域性情感群体，而非五个独立的国家群体。情感总体呈压倒性负面，国家间情感结构相似，但存在区域差异，如伊拉克因悲伤情绪而独树一帜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解阿拉伯YouTube情感公众的情绪基调，探究不同国家社群之间的情感异同，以及情感表达如何反映政治现实，对中东地区的公共外交具有启示意义。

Method: 收集67,725条围绕也门、沙特、伊拉克、约旦和叙利亚社会政治话题的YouTube评论，采用统一的情绪与情感分析流水线进行处理和分析。

Result: 研究表明存在一个单一的区域性情感公众而非五个独立的国家情感群体；所有国家语料库的情感均压倒性为负面，国家层面情感特征在结构上相似；共享基调中存在区域变异，尤其体现在与政治行为体和跨境历史创伤人物相关的讨论，以及伊拉克独特的悲伤情绪；情感基调差异与真实政治经历而非固定类别相关，如美国相关内容的情感倾向随美国是否直接军事介入而变化。

Conclusion: 阿拉伯YouTube情感公众的情感基调是共享的、具有历史层次性和地理条件性的，这一发现对中东地区的公共外交具有实际意义。

Abstract: What is the emotional register of Arabic YouTube's affective publics? To investigate this, we analyzed 67,725 YouTube comments collected around socio-political topics associated with Yemen, Saudi Arabia, Iraq, Jordan, and Syria using a unified sentiment-and-emotion pipeline. Our results profile a single regional affective public rather than five separate national ones. Sentiment is overwhelmingly negative across all five country-oriented corpora, and the country-level emotion profiles are structurally similar. This shared register still accommodates some regional variations: discourse is organized around country-level political actors and cross-border historical trauma figures, and grief singularizes Iraq from the other countries. The differences in emotional register also tracks lived political causes rather than fixed categories, which we observe from patterns of the valence of US-related content, that tracks the presence or absence of direct US military engagement. Our work shows that the emotional register of Arabic YouTube's affective publics is a shared one that is historically layered and geographically conditioned, which has implications for public diplomacy in the MENA region.

</details>


### [197] [Multilingual Agent System for Inclusive Wildfire Evacuation Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03301)
*Shruti Kulkarni,Lynn Tong,Aditi Namboodiripad,Chelyah Miller,Helen Lin,Peeyush Patel,Bogdan Bistriceanu,Diane Myung-kyung Woodbridge*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: BEACON是一个面向英语能力有限人群的多语言野火疏散引导服务系统，提供导航路线、个性化检查清单和聊天机器人功能，支持动态语言切换以服务边缘化社区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火季节比1970年代延长了84天，但美国超过80%的紧急消息仅以英语发布，导致2600万英语能力有限人群面临信息不对称和意识不足的问题，无法获得公平的紧急疏散指导。

Method: 系统整合来自Watch Duty的火场边界、疏散令状态和避难所数据，结合NOAA的实时GPS和天气数据，使用XGBoost动态调度评估模型刷新频率以预测火灾危险等级。用户接近火场时，通过多边形避让路由管线生成疏散路线并发送警报。系统还提供上下文感知的多语言智能体对话功能，并根据用户输入动态生成和核对个性化提醒清单，界面语言可根据用户最近使用的语言自动切换。

Result: 该系统能够为用户提供全面的个性化疏散指导，包括预测火灾危险、生成避让路线、提供多语言交互支持，并根据火灾进展动态更新评估模型。

Conclusion: BEACON通过多语言、个性化和动态化的疏散服务，致力于弥合紧急信息传播中的语言鸿沟，更好地服务边缘化社区在野火等紧急情况下的安全需求。

Abstract: Wildfire seasons have become 84 days longer in the current days than in the 1970s, causing enormous threats to one's financial status and short- and long-term health. During the fire, public agencies send out emergency messages to provide warnings and orders. Although 26 million people in the US have limited English proficiency, over 80% of those messages are only delivered in English, which can cause disproportionate information distribution and awareness. In order to better serve marginalized communities during emergencies, the authors developed BEACON, a service that provides comprehensive and personalized evacuation guidance, including navigation routes, personalized checklists, and a chatbot in the language that a user uses. Our current system ingests data including fire perimeter information, evacuation order status, and shelter information from Watch Duty. When a user is within a certain proximity from the fire, the system utilizes real-time GPS locations and nearby weather data from the National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration (NOAA) to predict fire danger levels. The assessment model refreshment are dynamically scheduled based on fire progress and trends using XGBoost. If the location has a likelihood of fire danger, the system sends alerts with evacuation routes outputted from a polygon-avoidant routing pipeline. The application provides a context-aware multilingual agent that users can communicate with and is tightly connected to other features of the application. In addition, based on data that the user entered, the system dynamically generates and checks off personalized reminder items to provide an organized evacuation plan. The system's user interface dynamically changes its language settings based on the language the user most recently used in either setting or chatbot conversation for all the application elements.

</details>


### [198] [The 5P Reflection Model for Education in the Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03413)
*Rajan Kadel,Samar Shailendra,Islam Mohammad Tahidul,Urvashi Rahul Saxena,Aakanksha Sharma,Sabitra Kaphle*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于生成式人工智能时代的反思模型——5P模型（目的、过程、产品、陷阱与计划），旨在解决传统反思模型无法应对GenAI带来的学术诚信和认知参与挑战的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球高校在将GenAI纳入课程时面临学术诚信和教学研究方面的挑战，传统反思模型难以验证学生反思的真实性，亟需一个GenAI感知的反思模型来应对这些挑战。

Method: 采用基于设计的研究方法，辅以批判性探究方法，分析现有反思模型在GenAI时代的表现，并考察GenAI的技术层面，识别现有模型的不足与空白。

Result: 文献调查显示GenAI时代对反思的需求更大，但现有反思模型缺乏应对GenAI引入问题的策略。提出标准的GenAI感知反思模型——5P模型（目的、过程、产品、陷阱和计划）。

Conclusion: 5P模型能够满足GenAI时代更大的反思需求，弥补现有反思模型的局限，并纳入GenAI的技术层面考量，为教育领域整合GenAI提供支持。

Abstract: Contributions: A reflection model suitable for the era of Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) is introduced. The proposed model is an integrated model that extracts features from various existing models and also incorporates technological aspects of GenAI.
  Background: Universities worldwide are facing challenges in adopting GenAI into their curricula, as it has impacted academic integrity and the scholarship of teaching and research. Traditional reflection models are struggling to authenticate student reflection as GenAI is incorporated in education. This requires a GenAI-aware model to enable the opportunities that address the associated challenges with GenAI.
  Research Questions: Does the academic ecosystem require a GenAI-aware reflection model to adopt GenAI into education? How to make a reflection model structured to ensure student authenticity and cognitive engagement within a GenAI-aware learning environment?
  Methodology: This study employs a design-based research methodology, assisted by a critical inquiry approach, to analyse existing reflection models in the era of GenAI and examine the technological aspects of GenAI. Additionally, it identifies a gap and the absence of a comprehensive reflection model that enhances reflection in the era of GenAI and supports the effective integration of GenAI in education.
  Findings: The literature survey indicates there is a greater need for reflection in the era of GenAI to ensure learning, but the existing reflection models lack proper strategies to manage the problem introduced by GenAI. A standard GenAI-aware reflection model, called 5P (Purpose, Process, Product, Pitfalls and Plan), is proposed i) to manage greater demand for reflection, ii) to address the limitations of the existing reflection models, iii) to consider technological aspects of GenAI.

</details>


### [199] [Open WebXR versus Commercial Game Engines: A Socio-Technical Position Analysis for an Open, Sustainable, and Interoperable Metaverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03666)
*Luca Turchet,Michel Buffa*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判性地比较了开放WebXR技术与传统商业游戏引擎在元宇宙开发中的优劣，主张不应将两者对立，而应视为一个连续光谱：WebXR更适合追求可访问性、互操作性和长期可维护性的公共应用，而商业引擎更适合追求高性能、深度硬件集成和大规模生产的XR产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前绝大多数XR应用依赖商业游戏引擎和专有应用商店开发与分发，与元宇宙所声称的"持久、互操作、具身"愿景存在张力。作者希望揭示这种开放性主张与实际平台依赖之间的矛盾，并为不同应用场景提供技术选型的批判性视角。

Method: 采用批判性分析框架，从XR硬件、软件架构、开发者工作流、治理、伦理和可持续性六个维度系统对比WebXR技术与商业游戏引擎管线的差异与利弊。

Result: 研究发现：WebXR为教育、科研、文化遗产、原型设计和公共利益应用提供了更可访问、设备无关且制度上可持续的元宇宙路径；而商业引擎在图形密集型、低延迟、深度集成和大规模XR产品方面仍具明显优势。

Conclusion: WebXR与商业引擎的选择不应被框定为二选一的对抗，而应视为连续光谱上的位置选择。决策应基于具体项目的核心目标——若优先考虑可访问性、互操作性和长期可维护性则选WebXR，若优先考虑性能、平台硬件访问和生产级工具链则选原生引擎。

Abstract: The Metaverse is often framed as a persistent, interoperable, and embodied network of virtual and augmented environments. Yet, most contemporary XR applications are developed through commercial game engines and distributed through proprietary app stores, creating tensions between openness and platform dependency. This paper critically examines open WebXR technologies with conventional commercial game-engine pipelines, with particular attention to XR hardware, software architectures, developer workflows, governance, ethics, and sustainability. We argue that WebXR may provide a viable route toward a more accessible, device-independent, and institutionally sustainable Metaverse, especially for education, research, cultural heritage, prototyping, and public-interest applications. At the same time, commercial engines remain advantageous for graphically intensive, low-latency, deeply integrated, and large-scale XR products. The paper concludes that the choice should not be framed as WebXR versus engines, but as a continuum: WebXR is preferable when accessibility, interoperability, low-friction deployment, and long-term maintainability are primary goals, whereas native engines remain preferable when performance, platform-specific hardware access, and production-grade tooling dominate.

</details>


### [200] [Bridging Formal and Perceived Fairness: Development of an Interdisciplinary Framework in Algorithmic Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03853)
*Maike Lindermayr,Mattia Cerrato,Luisa Hübner,Johannes Kraus*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概念框架，旨在弥合计算机科学视角的形式化算法公平性与社会科学视角的感知公平性之间的鸿沟，强调公平不仅是技术属性，更是受认知启发、心智模型和社会技术因素影响的主观判断，主张通过用户中心的评估方法整合计算公平审计与利益相关者感知评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法公平研究被计算机科学主导，过度强调形式化公平指标和偏见缓解策略，忽略了公平本质上是主观且情境敏感的，用户的公平感知可能与算法满足的技术公平标准严重脱节，导致系统缺乏信任、接受度和合法性。

Method: 采用三管齐下的研究方法：(1)理论文献综合；(2)跨学科研讨会；(3)利益相关者访谈，以此构建并验证一个概念框架，将形式化算法公平、感知公平/信任/技术接受度以及塑造人类判断的社会技术条件三者进行整合。

Result: 本文为工作进展中的概念框架，尚未呈现最终实验成果，但旨在为评估方法提供指导，使其能同时整合计算公平审计与用户中心评估，为设计公平感知感知的、以人为本的算法系统提供依据。

Conclusion: 公平应当是计算指标与人类主观判断的有机结合，算法系统需支持受影响利益相关者形成知情且校准良好的公平判断，从而在技术上满足公平要求的同时获得用户的信任与合法性认可。

Abstract: While fairness has become a central concern in research on algorithmic systems, the field remains predominantly shaped by Computer Science, resulting in a strong emphasis on formal fairness metrics and bias mitigation strategies. Nevertheless, this focus may obscure a fundamental challenge: fairness is not merely a technical property, but a subjective, context-sensitive human judgment shaped by cognitive heuristics, mental models, normative expectations, and sociotechnical factors. Crucially, users' perceptions of fairness may diverge substantially from the fairness criteria an algorithm formally satisfies; a system may meet predefined technical fairness requirements yet still be perceived as unjust by decision-affected stakeholders. In such cases, the system fails on a fundamental dimension: it will not be trusted, accepted, or considered legitimate. Taking a user-centered design perspective, this paper presents a work-in-progress conceptual framework that bridges Computer Science approaches to formal algorithmic fairness with normative and Social Science fairness approaches regarding perceived fairness, trust, and technology acceptance, embedding both within the sociotechnical conditions that shape human judgment. Through (1) theoretical literature synthesis, (2) interdisciplinary workshops, and (3) stakeholder interviews, the project aims to inform evaluation approaches that integrate computational fairness audits with user-centered assessments and guide the design of fairness-aware, human-centered algorithmic systems that support informed, well-calibrated fairness judgments by those affected.

</details>


### [201] [Making Gender-Inclusive Practices Actionable: Evaluating a Research-Informed Computing Education Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03936)
*Alina Berry,Susan McKeever,Brenda Murphy,Sarah Jane Delany*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为TechMate的基于网络的工具包，旨在帮助计算机教育工作者实施性别包容性举措。该工具包定义了25多项行动并提供案例研究和实施资源。通过对爱尔兰八所大学的18名计算机教育工作者的评估，发现TechMate在实用性和可用性方面获得高度评价，超过60%的参与者对所有属性给予积极反馈。主题分析还揭示了女性学生孤立、制度变革阻力、缺乏女性榜样等结构性障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机领域的性别失衡是全球性关注问题，大学是解决此问题的重要环节。虽然已有研究确定了支持弱势学生群体的实践，但将这些证据转化为可操作的指导仍然具有挑战性。因此需要开发工具帮助教育工作者实施性别包容性举措。

Method: 本文首先开发了TechMate——一个基于网络的工具包，提供超过25项从运营到战略层面的行动、案例研究和实施资源。然后通过18名来自爱尔兰八所大学的不同资历的计算机教育工作者进行评估，使用九个研究驱动属性（四项有用性指标：新颖性、相关性、可信度、可操作性；五项可用性指标：有效性、美观性、导航、术语、用户满意度）。数据使用混合方法分析：演绎编码、定量情感评分和归纳主题分析。

Result: 教育工作者对TechMate的用户满意度评价很高，并赞扬其新颖性和可信度，超过60%的参与者对所有属性给予积极评价，表明研究信息化的工具可以为实际使用而设计。主题分析还揭示了其他挑战：女性学生孤立、制度变革阻力、缺乏女性榜样，同时提出了解决方案如利用学生大使和培养支持性学习环境。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于研究的工具可以成功设计用于实际应用场景，TechMate在帮助计算机教育者实施性别包容性实践方面具有价值。同时，评估过程揭示了高等教育中性别包容性实践面临的结构性障碍，这些发现可为未来干预措施提供参考。

Abstract: The persistent gender imbalance in computing remains a global concern, and universities offer a key part of the pipeline to address it. Although research has identified practices that support under-represented student groups, translating this evidence into actionable guidance remains challenging. This paper first presents a novel web- based toolkit (TechMate) designed to address this gap by helping computing educators implement gender- inclusive initiatives through practical research-informed guidance. The toolkit defines over 25 actions, ranging from operational to strategic, and provides case studies and implementation resources. Second, this work reports on the evaluation of TechMate, capturing educators first impressions of its usefulness and usability through authentic tasks, and eliciting unanticipated insights about structural barriers to gender-inclusive practice in computing higher education. Eighteen computing educators of varying seniority from eight universities in Ireland assessed the toolkit using nine research-driven attributes: four usefulness measures (novelty, relevance, trustworthiness, actionability) and five usability measures (effectiveness, aesthetics, navigation, terminology, user satisfaction). Data was analysed using mixed methods: deductive coding, quantitative sentiment scoring, and inductive thematic analysis. Educators rated TechMate highly for user satisfaction and praised its novelty and trustworthiness, with over 60% of participants reporting positively on all attributes, suggesting that research -informed tools can be designed for real-world use. Thematic analysis highlighted other challenges, including women students isolation, institutional resistance to change, and the scarcity of women role models, while also surfacing proposed solutions such as leveraging student ambassadors and fostering supportive learning environments.

</details>


### [202] [Shifting from Injection to Interaction: Rethinking Web Security in the Age of LLMs and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03999)
*Nivedita Singh,Alsharif Abuadbba,Yansong Gao,Surya Nepal,Hyoungshick Kim*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对LLM增强的Web应用安全进行统一综述，分析LLM如何放大传统Web漏洞（如XSS），并引入提示注入等新型威胁。论文覆盖客户端、服务端和流水线三个层面，评估现有防御并扩展NIST和ISO/IEC AI安全框架，提出LLM感知的监控与控制框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将Web安全和LLM安全分开处理，缺乏对二者交互威胁的统一分析。随着LLM深度融入Web应用和浏览器代理，传统漏洞被放大、新型漏洞跨应用传播，需要系统性理解传统与LLM特定威胁在系统生命周期中的相互作用。

Method: 作为综述论文，采用统一分析视角，从客户端、服务端和流水线三个层次考察LLM对Web漏洞的放大效应，基于NIST和ISO/IEC AI安全框架扩展安全需求，并提出了一个整合语义输入验证、提示完整性保护、输出隔离、代理治理和运行时监控的LLM感知监控与控制框架。

Result: 识别出三个未解决的挑战：对抗性自然语言指令、自主代理安全、以及通过持续监控与适应实现的部署后安全。提出的LLM感知框架为应对这些挑战提供了结构化解决方案方向。

Conclusion: 该统一视角刻画了不断演变的威胁图谱，指出了保护AI使能的Web系统的未来方向，强调了将传统Web安全与现代LLM安全相结合的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming integral to web applications and browser agents, transforming online interactions while introducing new attack vectors and reshaping longstanding web vulnerabilities. Classical threats such as cross-site scripting (XSS) can be amplified through LLM-mediated interactions, while LLM-specific vulnerabilities can propagate across web applications, introducing attacks such as prompt injection. Securing modern web systems therefore requires understanding interactions between traditional and LLM-specific threats across the system lifecycle. Unlike prior surveys treating web and LLM security separately, this survey provides a unified analysis of how LLMs amplify web vulnerabilities across client-side, server-side, and pipeline layers while evaluating defenses and their limitations. The analysis examines extending NIST and ISO/IEC AI security frameworks to the security needs of LLM-enabled web environments. Three unresolved challenges are identified: adversarial natural-language instructions, autonomous agent security, and post-deployment security through continuous monitoring and adaptation. An LLM-aware monitoring and control framework is proposed, integrating semantic input validation, prompt integrity protection, output isolation, agent governance, and runtime monitoring. This unified perspective characterizes the evolving threat landscape and outlines future directions for secure AI-enabled web systems.

</details>


### [203] [Decreasing Digital Distraction in College Students: Associated Online Learning Strategies Identified by Unsupervised Data Mining Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04125)
*Hui Shi,Ran Bi,Xi Lin,Yan Dai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究运用无监督数据挖掘技术（关联规则挖掘与聚类分析），基于530名大学生的数据，识别出与较低数字分心水平相关的有效学习策略。结果表明自我调节学习策略（目标设定、环境建构、时间管理）与低分心水平最一致地共现，师生及生生内容参与策略和技术能力也相关，而同伴求助和生生互动策略较少出现在低分心画像中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字工具的普及在带来教育便利的同时也引入了显著挑战，尤其是数字分心会损害学业表现，尤其在在线学习情境中问题更为突出。因此需要识别能够帮助降低数字分心的有效学习策略。

Method: 采用无监督数据挖掘方法，具体包括关联规则挖掘和聚类分析，对530名大学生的数据进行策略模式识别与分心水平画像分析。

Result: 自我调节学习策略（目标设定、环境建构、时间管理）与低数字分心水平最一致共现；师生互动与内容参与策略以及技术能力也与低分心相关；而同伴求助和生生参与策略较少出现在低分心画像中。

Conclusion: 研究结果为教育者设计针对性干预措施提供了可操作的建议，以营造专注且高效的在线学习环境。

Abstract: The proliferation of digital tools in education offers numerous benefits but also introduces significant challenges, notably digital distractions that hinder academic performance, especially in online learning contexts. This study employed unsupervised data mining techniques, specifically association rule mining and clustering analysis, to identify effective learning strategies associated with lower levels of digital distractions among college students. Data from 530 participants revealed that self-regulated learning strategies (i.e., goal setting, environment structuring, and time management) co-occurred most consistently with lower digital distractions. Additionally, learner-instructor and learner-content engagement strategies, as well as technical competencies, also tended to appear in the same profiles as lower distraction. Interestingly, reliance on peer help-seeking and learner-learner engagement strategies appeared less often in those lower distraction profiles. These findings offer actionable implications for educators to design targeted interventions that foster focused and productive online learning environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型的unembedding矩阵中存在一个特定方向，编码了训练语料的unigram分布，充当模型在信息不足时的贝叶斯先验（称为"无知方向"）。将该方向投影到最终预测状态可得到每个token的先验载荷因子λ，该因子随上下文信息量增加而下降，且投影分解恰好对应tempered贝叶斯更新的两个因子。该方向具有因果活性，调节λ可控制预测向先验的偏移程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型在信息线索极少时的预测行为，并揭示其内部表示中先验知识的几何结构，从而建立语言模型预测行为与贝叶斯推理之间的形式化联系。

Method: 通过分析四种模型家族（Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia，0.4B至405B参数）的unembedding矩阵，识别编码unigram分布的方向；将最终预测状态投影到此方向获得先验载荷因子λ；利用几何投影将预测状态分解为与tempered贝叶斯更新对应的两个正交分量；通过调节λ进行因果干预实验验证该方向的实际作用。

Result: 该"无知方向"在所有四种模型家族中普遍存在；λ随上下文信息增加而稳定下降；投影分解与tempered贝叶斯更新（unigram先验的λ次幂与上下文似然）精确对应；大规模模型在高上下文场景下通常表现出更低的先验依赖；通过干预λ可在KL散度意义上引导预测趋向或远离unigram先验。

Conclusion: 语言模型的预测机制可以被理解为一种几何化的tempered贝叶斯更新，其中unigram先验编码在unembedding矩阵的单一方向中，λ作为可解释且跨模型可比较的标量，量化了模型在不同上下文信息量下对先验的依赖程度，且该机制是因果有效的。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [205] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"方程重铸"(equation recast)方法，将参数化算子学习重构为单一正则算子学习，通过从控制方程解析推导参数引起的算子变化并将其吸收为有效源项，实现跨参数区间的零样本预测，并在核聚变托卡马克模拟等多参数、非线性和奇异PDE场景中验证了外推能力、异构数据整合和可监控推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的参数化模型需要大量覆盖输入函数和物理参数的数据，成本高昂；且当模型面对训练分布之外的参数时可能"静默失败"，缺乏可检测的失败信号。这些限制阻碍了神经PDE求解器的可复用性和可靠性。

Method: 核心思想是将参数化算子学习重铸为单一正则(canonical)算子学习：参数引起的算子变化从控制方程中解析推导出来，并吸收为等效源项(effective sources)。这样只需学习一个统一的算子，而非针对每个参数配置单独建模。具体包括：(1) 零样本跨参数预测；(2) 通过共享正则表示整合稀疏异构数据集；(3) 利用重铸迭代的收敛损失作为内部预警信号检测失败。框架在托卡马克核聚变模拟中将四种设备几何的电子温度数据通过正则域映射统一到一个联合训练的算子中。

Result: 在多种PDE场景（多参数、非线性、奇异）中支持外推和跨参数泛化；能够以共享正则表示整合稀疏异构数据集；通过收敛失败作为内部警告信号实现可监测推理。在高保真托卡马克模拟中，成功将四种设备几何的电子温度数据统一到单一联合训练的算子中。

Conclusion: Equation recast为实现可复用的神经PDE求解器提供了一条新路径，兼具方程引导的迁移学习、数据效率和可监控推理能力，有望推动参数化算子学习在实际工程（如核聚变）中的可靠应用。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [206] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对协作学习（从欧几里得数据到图结构数据）进行了全面的综述研究，梳理了其基本框架、三类数据分布场景及对应的挑战，并指出了开放问题与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习（数据收集、训练、推理集中于单一位置）面临可扩展性和隐私等根本性限制；现有协作学习研究多聚焦于欧几里得数据（如图像、文本），难以处理现实世界中普遍存在的图结构关系数据，而图上的协作学习机遇与挑战尚未被充分探索。

Method: 文章作为综述，首先从三个核心维度（学习有效性、效率、隐私保护）回顾协作学习在欧几里得数据上的基础原理；随后将讨论扩展到图结构数据，提出图分布场景的分类法，刻画统计异质性，并建立标准化的问题定义与算法框架。

Result: 系统性地整合了从欧几里得到图结构数据的协作学习领域，提出了一套包含图分布场景分类、异质性表征、问题定义及算法框架的完整体系。

Conclusion: 协作学习从欧几里得数据向图结构数据的延伸是一个新兴且重要的方向；作者系统指出了当前面临的开放挑战，并提出了有前景的未来研究方向。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [207] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于证据的头级功能组织理论，通过RFIS和RPD两种干预指标揭示了RoPE Transformer中检索头与位置头的完整分类学（由Global Positional Band界定），并据此提出两个设计原则，实例化为Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH)，即以无位置编码的全注意力处理全局检索、线性注意力处理局部位置建模，在较低FA/LA比例下显著改善零样本长上下文外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合全注意力(FA)与线性注意力(LA)的架构日益流行，但两者的分配方式仍依赖启发式方法，缺乏证据支撑。作者希望从RoPE Transformer中学习到的头级功能组织中寻找经验基础，以指导混合架构设计。

Method: 提出两种干预指标：RFIS（RoPE Frequency Importance Score）衡量每个频率对注意力分布的影响，RPD（RoPE Positional Dependence）隔离对旋转位置调制的依赖性。在Qwen3系列和Llama3.1上验证了检索头与位置头的完整分类学，识别的中低频带边界被命名为Global Positional Band (GPBand)。基于此提出两个原则：(1)位置建模应仅局部运作，全局访问通过位置无关的检索实现；(2)两种功能按头粒度分配并伴随层特定配置。基于原则设计了HwH架构，使用NoPE FA做全局检索、LA做局部位置建模。

Result: 在FA与LA比例低于1:3的情况下，HwH保持了强大的语言建模和常识推理能力，同时改善了检索性能，并显著增强了比Transformer、LA及层级混合基线更优的零样本长上下文外推能力。消融实验验证了两个原则及组件角色的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于头级功能组织的原则化混合架构设计是未来基础模型的有前景方向，研究中发现的分析可能揭示了零样本长度外推失败的一个潜在原因。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [208] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL)，一种通过优化策略超出随机奖励阈值的对数概率来直接最大化高收益覆盖率的新强化学习方法，仅需对advantage函数做简单修改即可兼容现有RL管线，在物体定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化等任务上优于标准平均奖励优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习优化平均奖励，但平均奖励掩盖了一个关键区别：两个策略可能有相同均值奖励，但产生稀有高收益rollout的概率差异很大。随着训练和推理时采样数量增加，收益取决于在高质量结果上保留概率质量。因此需要直接优化这种覆盖率，而非仅仅期望奖励。

Method: TailRL将连续奖励转化为一族二值成功事件：对每个奖励阈值，考虑策略超过该阈值的概率。目标是最大化超出随机选择奖励阈值的对数概率。梯度赋予稀有高收益rollout更大权重，可解释为Best-of-k梯度的混合。实现上仅需对advantage函数进行简单修改，即可兼容现有强化学习流程。

Result: 在物体定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化四个任务上，TailRL利用稀有高收益训练样本避免次优解，并在推理时随着采样数量增加获得更大的性能收益。

Conclusion: TailRL通过优化尾部覆盖率而非平均奖励，能够更好地利用稀有高收益样本，产出在推理采样更多时获益更大的模型，是一种简单且广泛适用的强化学习改进方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold.
